ShellX Cut third-party notices
==============================

ShellX Cut itself is licensed under the MIT License (LICENSE). The assets bundled inside the installers
(face-detection models and the FFmpeg guidance) are recorded in NOTICE. This file lists the libraries
that are compiled into the ShellX Cut programs or bundled into its user interface, with their licence
identifiers, copyright lines and the full licence texts, as the licences require.

Scope
- Rust: every crate in the normal dependency tree of the engine (cutd) and of the desktop shell, for the
  Windows, macOS and Linux builds. Build-only crates (build scripts) and test-only crates are not shipped
  and are not listed. ShellX Cut's own crates are not listed.
- npm: the production dependency closure of the user interface (a superset of what the bundler keeps).
- The WebDriver test plugin (app/desktop/src-tauri/test-support) is compiled only into the diagnostic test
  variant used for automated testing; it is never part of a published installer and is not listed.
- Not listed here: the captions Python packages (Install captions downloads them at the person's request;
  they are not shipped with the app) and FFmpeg (downloaded at first run, never bundled).

Generated by the ShellX Cut release tooling from the lockfiles below; do not edit by hand.
